    The live test replaces crawl, index and rank with tiny fakes using monkeypatch, so it checks only the loop: index first, then repeat until the crawl ends. That works because cmd_live looks the functions up by name each time it calls them.
  11. Fix PageRank when two pages end at one URL (whole updated seekr/rank.py)
    seekr/rank.py
    """Authority: PageRank over the links between crawled pages."""
import json

import numpy as np

SCHEMA = "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pagerank (doc_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, score REAL NOT NULL);"
DAMPING = 0.85  # chance the "random surfer" follows a link instead of jumping to a random page


def link_graph(conn):
    """Return (doc_ids, edges): edges are (from, to) positions in doc_ids, one per linked pair of pages."""
    rows = conn.execute(
        """SELECT p.url, p.final_url, p.outlinks, IFNULL(d.duplicate_of, d.id) AS doc_id
           FROM pages p JOIN documents d ON d.url = p.url"""
    ).fetchall()
    doc_of = {}  # any URL we crawled (before or after redirects) -> its document; duplicates -> the original
    for row in rows:
        doc_of[row["url"]] = doc_of[row["final_url"]] = row["doc_id"]
    doc_ids = sorted({row["doc_id"] for row in rows})  # not doc_of.values(): two pages can redirect to one URL
    position = {doc_id: i for i, doc_id in enumerate(doc_ids)}
    edges = set()
    for row in rows:
        source = position[row["doc_id"]]
        for link in json.loads(row["outlinks"] or "[]"):
            if link in doc_of and position[doc_of[link]] != source:
                edges.add((source, position[doc_of[link]]))
    return doc_ids, sorted(edges)


def pagerank(n, edges, damping=DAMPING, tolerance=1e-10, max_iterations=100):
    """Power iteration. Returns one score per node; the scores add up to 1."""
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    edges = np.array(edges, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1, 2)
    source, target = edges[:, 0], edges[:, 1]
    out_degree = np.bincount(source, minlength=n)
    rank = np.full(n, 1 / n)
    for _ in range(max_iterations):
        passed_on = np.bincount(target, weights=rank[source] / out_degree[source], minlength=n)
        dangling = rank[out_degree == 0].sum()  # pages with no links share their score with everyone
        new_rank = (1 - damping) / n + damping * (passed_on + dangling / n)
        if np.abs(new_rank - rank).sum() < tolerance:
            return new_rank
        rank = new_rank
    return rank


def compute_pagerank(conn):
    """Rebuild the pagerank table from scratch. Returns (number of pages, number of links)."""
    conn.executescript(SCHEMA)
    doc_ids, edges = link_graph(conn)
    scores = pagerank(len(doc_ids), edges)
    conn.execute("DELETE FROM pagerank")
    conn.executemany("INSERT INTO pagerank VALUES (?, ?)", zip(doc_ids, scores.tolist()))
    conn.commit()
    return len(doc_ids), len(edges)
    The third real crash, which only appeared at about 37,000 pages: KeyError in link_graph. Page /a and page /old-a, which redirects to /a, are two crawled pages with two documents. Both write doc_of["/a"], so the second one wins, and the first document disappears from doc_of.values(). Then position[row["doc_id"]] can't find it. Building doc_ids from every row fixes it. Bugs like this hide in small test crawls and show up at scale, which is why we keep running bigger crawls.
  12. Test it (whole updated tests/test_rank.py)
    tests/test_rank.py
    import numpy as np

from seekr import db, rank


def test_cycle_gives_equal_scores():
    scores = rank.pagerank(3, [(0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 0)])
    assert np.allclose(scores, 1 / 3)


def test_most_linked_page_wins_and_scores_sum_to_one():
    scores = rank.pagerank(4, [(1, 0), (2, 0), (3, 0)])  # page 0 has no links of its own (dangling)
    assert scores.argmax() == 0
    assert np.isclose(scores.sum(), 1.0)
    assert np.allclose(scores[1:], scores[1])  # 1, 2 and 3 are in the same position


def test_known_values_for_a_small_graph():
    # Worked by hand: B and C link to A, A links to B. Solve r = 0.05 + 0.85 * (incoming shares).
    a, b, c = rank.pagerank(3, [(1, 0), (2, 0), (0, 1)])
    assert np.isclose(c, 0.05)
    assert np.isclose(a, 0.05 + 0.85 * (b + c))
    assert np.isclose(b, 0.05 + 0.85 * a)


def test_link_graph_follows_redirects_and_merges_duplicates(tmp_path):
    conn = db.connect(tmp_path / "t.db")
    db.save_page(conn, "https://x.com/a", "https://x.com/a", 200, "A", "", ["https://x.com/old-b", "https://x.com/a"])
    db.save_page(conn, "https://x.com/old-b", "https://x.com/b", 200, "B", "", ["https://x.com/a-copy"])
    db.save_page(conn, "https://x.com/a-copy", "https://x.com/a-copy", 200, "A", "", [])
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO documents (id, url) VALUES (1, 'https://x.com/a'), (2, 'https://x.com/old-b')")
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO documents (id, url, duplicate_of) VALUES (3, 'https://x.com/a-copy', 1)")
    doc_ids, edges = rank.link_graph(conn)
    assert doc_ids == [1, 2]
    assert edges == [(0, 1), (1, 0)]  # a -> b (via redirect) and b -> a (via the duplicate); no self-link


def test_compute_pagerank_stores_scores(tmp_path):
    conn = db.connect(tmp_path / "t.db")
    db.save_page(conn, "https://x.com/a", "https://x.com/a", 200, "A", "", ["https://x.com/b"])
    db.save_page(conn, "https://x.com/b", "https://x.com/b", 200, "B", "", [])
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO documents (id, url) VALUES (1, 'https://x.com/a'), (2, 'https://x.com/b')")
    assert rank.compute_pagerank(conn) == (2, 1)
    scores = dict(conn.execute("SELECT doc_id, score FROM pagerank").fetchall())
    assert scores[2] > scores[1]


def test_link_graph_survives_two_pages_ending_at_one_url(tmp_path):
    conn = db.connect(tmp_path / "t.db")
    db.save_page(conn, "https://x.com/a", "https://x.com/a", 200, "A", "", ["https://x.com/b"])
    db.save_page(conn, "https://x.com/old-a", "https://x.com/a", 200, "A", "", [])  # also redirects to /a
    db.save_page(conn, "https://x.com/b", "https://x.com/b", 200, "B", "", [])
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO documents (id, url) VALUES (1, 'https://x.com/a'), (2, 'https://x.com/old-a'),"
                 " (3, 'https://x.com/b')")
    doc_ids, edges = rank.link_graph(conn)  # used to raise KeyError: 1
    assert doc_ids == [1, 2, 3]
    The new last test builds exactly that situation. Against the old rank.py it fails with KeyError: 1, the same error live hit.
  13. Run all the tests
    python -m pytest -q
    72 passed
  14. Start live mode with bigger limits
    SEEKR_MAX_PAGES_PER_DOMAIN=5000 SEEKR_MAX_DEPTH=5 python -m seekr live
    This continues the crawl from the saved frontier, allows up to 5,000 pages per site and links up to 5 clicks deep, and indexes every 10 minutes. Leave it running. It ends only when every site runs out of new links. Then add seeds, or raise the limits, and start it again.
  15. In a second terminal, start the website
    source .venv/bin/activate && python -m seekr serve
    Search as usual. New pages appear on their own after each index run, with no restart.

Checkpoint

python -m seekr stats

While live runs, the counts keep growing. In our run, 15 sites and 4,086 pages grew to 80 seed sites and 86,490 pages (77,321 searchable after removing duplicates and empty pages) in about three hours. That's 402,180 chunks, with the GPU catching up on embeddings in the background. Then try a search from a new site, such as nasa mars, in the browser without restarting serve.

The crawl stops with ValueError: Invalid IPv6 URL
The try/except from step 3 is missing in normalize_url.
test_project_seeds_are_web_urls_without_repeats fails
Two URLs ended up on one line, or the same seed is listed twice.
sqlite3.OperationalError: database is locked
The timeout=60 or the PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL line is missing from db.connect.
JSONDecodeError: Invalid \escape during index
The try/except in extract_row is missing.
KeyError in link_graph during rank
doc_ids must come from every row, not from doc_of.values() (step 11).
New pages never show up in the website
Check that search() calls self.refresh(). Also check that live has finished at least one index run: look for "embedded:" in its output.
One site is still crawling long after the others finished
Usually a site with a long Crawl-delay. Stop with Ctrl+C, index what you have, and resume later.
A new site shows only 1 page done
Its pages may be built by JavaScript, so the HTML has few links. Check with curl -s URL | grep -c href.

Chapter 12 · Site importance & discovering new sites ready

Without users, how does a search engine know which sites matter? It asks the web. Every link is a vote. Here we count those votes per site, use them in ranking, and let Seekr find new sites to crawl by itself.

The problem: our PageRank (Chapter 6) only sees links between pages we crawled, and the crawler drops links that leave its seed sites. But most of the web's votes are exactly those links between sites: a BBC article linking to NASA, a blog linking to GitHub. We already store every page's links, including the ones we don't follow, so the votes are already in our database.

Two uses for the same counts:

  • Ranking: a result from a site that many other sites link to gets a small boost, called site authority.
  • Discovery: the most-linked sites we don't crawl yet are the best candidates to crawl next. From 80 seeds, our crawl linked to 33,795 different sites. 2,293 of them were linked from 3 or more of ours, and the top ones are what you'd hope: Wikipedia, GitHub, Stack Overflow, W3C, arXiv.

What users would add later: clicks, and whether people come back to the results. But click data needs privacy care, and it's easy to fake. Links plus a relevance model trained on real searches (Chapter 7's cross-encoder learned from about 500,000 real Bing queries in MS MARCO) get you most of the way without any users.

  1. Add the sites and discovered tables (whole updated seekr/db.py)
    seekr/db.py
    """SQLite storage: one file on disk, a few small tables."""
import json
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from seekr import config

SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS frontier (
    url    TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    host   TEXT NOT NULL,
    depth  INTEGER NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued'  -- queued, done, failed, error or blocked
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS frontier_next ON frontier (host, status, depth);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (
    url        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    final_url  TEXT,
    status     INTEGER,
    title      TEXT,
    html       TEXT,
    outlinks   TEXT,  -- JSON list of absolute URLs
    fetched_at TEXT
);

-- One row per crawled page. Duplicates and pages without main text get no chunks.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS documents (
    id           INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    url          TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    title        TEXT,
    description  TEXT,
    published    TEXT,
    language     TEXT,
    word_count   INTEGER,
    content_hash TEXT,
    duplicate_of INTEGER
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS documents_hash ON documents (content_hash);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chunks (
    id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    doc_id   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    position INTEGER NOT NULL,
    text     TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS embeddings (
    chunk_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    vector   BLOB NOT NULL  -- float32 bytes, length 1.0
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sites (
    site        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,  -- host without "www."
    linked_from INTEGER NOT NULL,  -- how many other sites link to it
    score       REAL NOT NULL      -- 0..1 on a log scale
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS discovered (
    site        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    seed        TEXT,              -- the homepage we crawl, or NULL if the site failed our checks
    linked_from INTEGER NOT NULL,
    checked_at  TEXT NOT NULL
);
"""


def connect(path=None):
    path = Path(path or config.DB_PATH)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=60)  # wait (up to 60 s) for another program's write instead of failing
    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")  # lets crawl, index and serve use the database at the same time
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    conn.executescript(SCHEMA)
    return conn


def enqueue(conn, urls, depth):
    rows = [(url, url.split("/")[2], depth) for url in urls]
    conn.executemany("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO frontier (url, host, depth) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", rows)


def next_url(conn, host):
    return conn.execute(
        "SELECT url, depth FROM frontier WHERE host = ? AND status = 'queued' ORDER BY depth LIMIT 1",
        (host,),
    ).fetchone()


def mark(conn, url, status):
    conn.execute("UPDATE frontier SET status = ? WHERE url = ?", (status, url))


def redirect_targets(conn, urls):
    """Where these URLs ended up after redirects, for the ones that were redirected."""
    marks = ",".join("?" * len(urls))
    sql = f"SELECT final_url FROM pages WHERE url IN ({marks}) AND final_url != url"
    return [row["final_url"] for row in conn.execute(sql, urls)]


def requeue_errors(conn):
    conn.execute("UPDATE frontier SET status = 'queued' WHERE status = 'error'")


def count_done(conn, host):
    sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM frontier WHERE host = ? AND status = 'done'"
    return conn.execute(sql, (host,)).fetchone()[0]


def save_page(conn, url, final_url, status, title, html, outlinks):
    fetched_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO pages VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        (url, final_url, status, title, html, json.dumps(outlinks), fetched_at),
    )


def host_stats(conn):
    return conn.execute(
        """SELECT host,
                  SUM(status = 'done')    AS done,
                  SUM(status = 'queued')  AS queued,
                  SUM(status = 'failed')  AS failed,
                  SUM(status = 'error')   AS error,
                  SUM(status = 'blocked') AS blocked
           FROM frontier GROUP BY host ORDER BY host"""
    ).fetchall()
    Two new tables at the end of the schema. sites holds each site's importance and is rebuilt on every run. discovered remembers every site we ever checked, including the ones that failed (seed is NULL), so we never check a site twice.
  2. Create the discovery module
    seekr/discover.py
    """Which sites matter (how many other sites link to them), and finding new sites to crawl."""
import asyncio
import json
import logging
import math
import re
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import httpx

from seekr import config, crawler

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
# Share buttons and link shorteners are linked from everywhere but have no articles for us.
SKIP_SITES = {
    "facebook.com", "twitter.com", "x.com", "linkedin.com", "instagram.com", "youtube.com", "youtu.be",
    "tiktok.com", "pinterest.com", "reddit.com", "whatsapp.com", "wa.me", "t.me", "threads.net", "bsky.app",
    "google.com", "goo.gl", "bit.ly", "t.co", "apple.com", "apps.apple.com", "play.google.com", "amazon.com",
    "docs.google.com", "drive.google.com", "groups.google.com", "maps.google.com", "gist.github.com",
    "web.archive.org", "vimeo.com", "news.ycombinator.com", "creativecommons.org",
}
HTML_LANG = re.compile(r'<html[^>]*\blang="?([a-zA-Z-]+)', re.I)


def site_of(url):
    host = urlsplit(url).netloc.lower()
    return host[4:] if host.startswith("www.") else host


def site_links(conn):
    """{site: set of the other crawled sites that link to it}, from every crawled page's links."""
    linked_from = defaultdict(set)
    for row in conn.execute("SELECT url, outlinks FROM pages"):
        source = site_of(row["url"])
        for link in json.loads(row["outlinks"] or "[]"):
            target = site_of(link)
            if target and target != source:
                linked_from[target].add(source)
    return linked_from


def compute_sites(conn):
    """Rebuild the sites table. A site linked from many different sites is probably important."""
    counts = {site: len(sources) for site, sources in site_links(conn).items()}
    top = math.log1p(max(counts.values(), default=1))
    conn.execute("DELETE FROM sites")
    conn.executemany("INSERT INTO sites VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                     [(site, n, math.log1p(n) / top) for site, n in counts.items()])
    conn.commit()
    return len(counts)


def found_seeds(conn):
    return [row["seed"] for row in conn.execute("SELECT seed FROM discovered WHERE seed IS NOT NULL")]


async def check_site(client, site):
    """The homepage URL if robots.txt lets us in and it's an English HTML page, else None."""
    robots = await crawler.load_robots(client, site)
    if robots is None or not robots.can_fetch(config.USER_AGENT, f"https://{site}/"):
        return None
    status, final_url, html = await crawler.fetch(client, f"https://{site}/")
    lang = HTML_LANG.search(html[:5000]) if html else None
    if not html or (lang and not lang.group(1).lower().startswith("en")):
        return None
    return final_url


async def check_sites(sites, transport=None):
    headers = {"User-Agent": config.USER_AGENT}
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(headers=headers, timeout=config.REQUEST_TIMEOUT, follow_redirects=True,
                                 transport=transport) as client:
        return await asyncio.gather(*(check_site(client, site) for site in sites))


def discover(conn, seeds, limit=None, transport=None):
    """Check the most-linked sites we don't crawl yet; remember each answer. Returns the new seeds."""
    known = {site_of(seed) for seed in seeds} | {row[0] for row in conn.execute("SELECT site FROM discovered")}
    rows = conn.execute("SELECT site, linked_from FROM sites WHERE linked_from >= ? ORDER BY linked_from DESC",
                        (config.DISCOVER_MIN_LINKS,))
    picked = [(site, n) for site, n in rows if site not in known and site not in SKIP_SITES]
    picked = picked[: limit or config.DISCOVER_PER_ROUND]
    results = asyncio.run(check_sites([site for site, _ in picked], transport))
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    conn.executemany("INSERT INTO discovered VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                     [(site, seed, n, now) for (site, n), seed in zip(picked, results)])
    conn.commit()
    added = [seed for seed in results if seed]
    log.info("discovered %d new sites (%d checked)", len(added), len(picked))
    return added
    • Sites vote with links. site_links reads every crawled page's links and collects, for each site, the set of other sites that link to it. A set counts each linking site once: one blog with 500 links to GitHub is one vote. That makes the score hard to fake, because you'd need many different sites, not many pages. Links inside a site don't count.
    • site_of drops www., so www.bbc.com and bbc.com are one site.
    • The score is log(1 + n) / log(1 + most). The log stops the giants (YouTube, Wikipedia) from flattening everyone else: 10 linking sites scores 0.56 when the top has 70, not 0.14.
    • Discovery takes the most-linked sites we don't crawl yet, skips share buttons and link shorteners (SKIP_SITES: linked from everywhere, nothing to read), and checks each one before crawling: does robots.txt allow our User-Agent on the homepage, is the homepage HTML, and is it English? This is the same robots check the crawler does, so discovery never sneaks past a site's rules. LinkedIn, for example, disallows all bots, so it would fail the check even without the skip list.
    • asyncio.gather checks all 20 sites at once. Each site is a different host, so that's still one request at a time per site.
  3. Let a crawl stop at a deadline (whole updated seekr/crawler.py)
    seekr/crawler.py
    """A small, polite, resumable web crawler."""
import asyncio
import logging
import time
from html.parser import HTMLParser
from urllib.robotparser import RobotFileParser

import httpx

from seekr import config, db
from seekr.urls import build_scopes, in_scope, normalize_url

RETRY_STATUSES = {None, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504}
log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class LinkParser(HTMLParser):
    """Collects the <title> text, every <a href> and every <iframe src> on a page."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.title, self.links, self.in_title = "", [], False

    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        attrs = dict(attrs)
        if tag == "a" and attrs.get("href"):
            self.links.append(attrs["href"])
        elif tag == "iframe" and attrs.get("src"):  # some docs load their table of contents this way
            self.links.append(attrs["src"])
        elif tag == "title":
            self.in_title = True

    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
        if tag == "title":
            self.in_title = False

    def handle_data(self, data):
        if self.in_title:
            self.title += data


def parse_page(html, base_url):
    parser = LinkParser()
    parser.feed(html)
    links = {normalize_url(href, base_url) for href in parser.links}
    return parser.title.strip(), sorted(link for link in links if link)


async def load_robots(client, host):
    """Missing robots.txt (4xx) means allow all. Unreachable (5xx, network error) returns None."""
    try:
        resp = await client.get(f"https://{host}/robots.txt")
    except httpx.HTTPError:
        return None
    if resp.status_code >= 500:
        return None
    robots = RobotFileParser()
    if resp.status_code >= 400:
        robots.allow_all = True
    else:
        robots.parse(resp.text.splitlines())
    return robots


async def fetch_once(client, url):
    """Return (status, final_url, html). html is None for non-HTML or oversized pages."""
    async with client.stream("GET", url) as resp:
        final_url = str(resp.url)
        if resp.status_code != 200 or "text/html" not in resp.headers.get("content-type", ""):
            return resp.status_code, final_url, None
        body = b""
        async for chunk in resp.aiter_bytes():
            body += chunk
            if len(body) > config.MAX_PAGE_BYTES:
                return resp.status_code, final_url, None
        return resp.status_code, final_url, body.decode(resp.charset_encoding or "utf-8", "replace")


async def fetch(client, url):
    """fetch_once, retried with exponential backoff (1s, 2s, ...) on network errors, 429 and 5xx."""
    for attempt in range(config.RETRIES + 1):
        try:
            status, final_url, html = await fetch_once(client, url)
        except httpx.HTTPError:
            status, final_url, html = None, url, None
        if status not in RETRY_STATUSES or attempt == config.RETRIES:
            return status, final_url, html
        await asyncio.sleep(2**attempt)


async def crawl_host(client, conn, host, scopes, until=None):
    """Crawl one host, one request at a time, waiting between requests (and stopping at `until`)."""
    robots = await load_robots(client, host)
    if robots is None:  # can't read the rules, so stay away from this host until the next run
        log.warning("%s: robots.txt unreachable, skipping this host until the next run", host)
        return
    delay = max(config.CRAWL_DELAY, robots.crawl_delay(config.USER_AGENT) or 0)
    while db.count_done(conn, host) < config.MAX_PAGES_PER_DOMAIN and (until is None or time.monotonic() < until):
        row = db.next_url(conn, host)
        if row is None:
            break
        url, depth = row["url"], row["depth"]
        if not robots.can_fetch(config.USER_AGENT, url):
            db.mark(conn, url, "blocked")
            continue
        status, final_url, html = await fetch(client, url)
        log.debug("%s %s", status, url)
        if depth == 0 and final_url != url:  # a seed redirected, e.g. /en/stable/ -> /en/6.1/
            scopes.extend(build_scopes([final_url]))
        if html:
            title, links = parse_page(html, final_url)
            db.save_page(conn, url, final_url, status, title, html, links)
            if depth < config.MAX_DEPTH:
                db.enqueue(conn, [link for link in links if in_scope(link, scopes)], depth + 1)
        if html:
            db.mark(conn, url, "done")
        else:  # no status means a network error: worth retrying on the next run
            db.mark(conn, url, "failed" if status else "error")
            log.info("%s %s", status or "network error", url)
        conn.commit()
        await asyncio.sleep(delay)
    log.info("%s: done (%d pages)", host, db.count_done(conn, host))


async def crawl(seeds, conn, transport=None, until=None):
    """Crawl all seed hosts at the same time (each host politely, one request at a time)."""
    seed_urls = [normalize_url(seed) for seed in seeds]
    scopes = build_scopes(seed_urls + db.redirect_targets(conn, seed_urls))
    db.enqueue(conn, seed_urls, depth=0)
    db.requeue_errors(conn)
    conn.commit()
    hosts = sorted({host for host, _ in scopes})
    headers = {"User-Agent": config.USER_AGENT}
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(
        headers=headers, timeout=config.REQUEST_TIMEOUT, follow_redirects=True, transport=transport
    ) as client:
        await asyncio.gather(*(crawl_host(client, conn, host, scopes, until) for host in hosts))
    crawl(..., until=...) passes a deadline down to every host. The loop stops when time.monotonic(), a clock that only goes forward, passes it. live uses this to crawl in rounds, and each new round includes the sites discovered during the last one. Everything not fetched stays queued in the frontier.
  4. Add the discovery settings (whole updated seekr/config.py)
    seekr/config.py
    """All settings in one place. Override any of them with an environment variable."""
import os
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

DB_PATH = Path(os.environ.get("SEEKR_DB", ROOT / "data" / "seekr.db"))
SEEDS_FILE = Path(os.environ.get("SEEKR_SEEDS", ROOT / "seeds.txt"))
CONTACT = os.environ.get("SEEKR_CONTACT", "")  # e.g. "Your Name; you@example.com". Some sites (Wikipedia) require it
USER_AGENT = os.environ.get("SEEKR_USER_AGENT", f"SeekrBot/0.1 ({CONTACT})" if CONTACT else "SeekrBot/0.1")
HOST = os.environ.get("SEEKR_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
PORT = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_PORT", "8000"))

# Crawler
MAX_DEPTH = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_MAX_DEPTH", "3"))
MAX_PAGES_PER_DOMAIN = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_MAX_PAGES_PER_DOMAIN", "300"))
LIVE_INTERVAL = float(os.environ.get("SEEKR_LIVE_INTERVAL", "600"))  # seconds between index runs in `live`
CRAWL_ROUND = float(os.environ.get("SEEKR_CRAWL_ROUND", "1800"))  # `live` restarts the crawl this often, with new sites
DISCOVER_MIN_LINKS = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_DISCOVER_MIN_LINKS", "3"))  # other sites that must link to a new one
DISCOVER_PER_ROUND = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_DISCOVER_PER_ROUND", "20"))  # sites checked per `discover`
CRAWL_DELAY = float(os.environ.get("SEEKR_CRAWL_DELAY", "1.0"))  # seconds between requests to one host
REQUEST_TIMEOUT = float(os.environ.get("SEEKR_REQUEST_TIMEOUT", "15"))
MAX_PAGE_BYTES = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_MAX_PAGE_BYTES", "5000000"))  # long Wikipedia articles are over 2 MB
RETRIES = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_RETRIES", "2"))

# Extraction
CHUNK_WORDS = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_CHUNK_WORDS", "300"))
CHUNK_OVERLAP = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_CHUNK_OVERLAP", "50"))

# Embeddings (runs on the GPU when there is one)
EMBED_MODEL = os.environ.get("SEEKR_EMBED_MODEL", "BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5")
EMBED_BATCH = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_EMBED_BATCH", "64"))
DEVICE = os.environ.get("SEEKR_DEVICE", "")  # "", "cuda", "mps" or "cpu"; "" picks the best one

# Search and relevance scoring
CANDIDATES = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_CANDIDATES", "100"))  # from each of BM25 and vectors
RERANK_TOP = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_RERANK_TOP", "30"))  # pages the cross-encoder reads per query
# MiniLM beat BAAI/bge-reranker-base on our eval (MRR 0.733 vs 0.648) and is 3x faster; try either.
RERANK_MODEL = os.environ.get("SEEKR_RERANK_MODEL", "cross-encoder/ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2")
RERANK_MAX_TOKENS = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_RERANK_MAX_TOKENS", "384"))
# Tuned on evals/queries.jsonl. Freshness is 0: docs show "last updated today", so dates mislead.
W_RELEVANCE, W_PAGERANK, W_FRESHNESS, W_EXACT = 0.90, 0.05, 0.0, 0.05
W_SITE = float(os.environ.get("SEEKR_W_SITE", "0.05"))  # how many other sites link to the result's site
THIN_PAGE_WORDS, THIN_PAGE_PENALTY = 50, 0.8

# REST API
RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE", "60"))  # per API key
CORS_ORIGINS = os.environ.get("SEEKR_CORS_ORIGINS", "*").split(",")  # websites allowed to call the API

# Website
UI_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE = int(os.environ.get("SEEKR_UI_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE", "30"))  # per visitor IP

# Operations
LOG_LEVEL = os.environ.get("SEEKR_LOG_LEVEL", "INFO")  # DEBUG shows every fetched URL


def read_seeds(path=None):
    """Return the seed URLs, skipping blank lines and # comments."""
    lines = Path(path or SEEDS_FILE).read_text().splitlines()
    return [line.strip() for line in lines if line.strip() and not line.startswith("#")]
    CRAWL_ROUND (30 minutes per crawl round), DISCOVER_MIN_LINKS (a new site must be linked from at least 3 of ours), DISCOVER_PER_ROUND (check 20 sites each time) and W_SITE, the weight of the new ranking signal. Two more changes at the top:
    • CONTACT goes into the User-Agent as SeekrBot/0.1 (Your Name; you@example.com). Wikipedia's User-Agent policy refuses bots (403) unless they say how to reach their operator. It comes from an environment variable, so your email never ends up in the code or in this guide.
    • MAX_PAGE_BYTES rises from 2 MB to 5 MB. Wikipedia's article on Adele is 2.1 MB of HTML, and the old limit silently skipped it. The page counted as "failed" with status 200, which is the clue.
  5. Use site importance in the final score (whole updated seekr/search.py)
    seekr/search.py
    """Hybrid search: keyword + semantic candidates, then a cross-encoder decides how relevant each page is."""
import html
import math
import re
from datetime import date
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import numpy as np
import torch
from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder

from seekr import config, discover, embed, index

CHUNK_SQL = """SELECT c.doc_id, c.text, d.url, d.title, d.published, d.language, d.word_count
               FROM chunks c JOIN documents d ON d.id = c.doc_id WHERE c.id = ?"""
STOPWORDS = {"the", "and", "for", "how", "can", "what", "with", "does", "are", "you", "your", "from", "that", "this",
             "when", "why", "which", "into", "about", "same"}
_reranker = None


def get_reranker():
    global _reranker
    if _reranker is None:
        device = embed.best_device()
        _reranker = CrossEncoder(config.RERANK_MODEL, device=device, max_length=config.RERANK_MAX_TOKENS)
        if device != "cpu":
            _reranker.model.half()
    return _reranker


def sigmoid(x):
    return 1 / (1 + np.exp(-x))


def rrf(rankings, k=60):
    """Reciprocal Rank Fusion. rankings: {name: [(id, score), ...] best first} -> (fused scores, ranks)."""
    fused, ranks = {}, {}
    for name, hits in rankings.items():
        for rank, (item, _) in enumerate(hits, start=1):
            fused[item] = fused.get(item, 0.0) + 1 / (k + rank)
            ranks.setdefault(item, {})[name] = rank
    return fused, ranks


def query_words(query):
    return re.findall(r"\w+", query.lower())


def highlight(text, query, width=300):
    """A window of text around the first query word, HTML-escaped, with query words wrapped in <mark>."""
    words = [re.escape(word) for word in query_words(query) if len(word) > 2 and word not in STOPWORDS]
    pattern = re.compile(r"\b(?:" + "|".join(words) + r")\w*", re.IGNORECASE) if words else None
    first = pattern.search(text) if pattern else None
    start = max(0, first.start() - 80) if first else 0
    start = text.find(" ", start) + 1 if start else 0  # begin on a whole word
    window = ("…" if start else "") + text[start : start + width] + ("…" if start + width < len(text) else "")
    parts, last = [], 0
    for match in pattern.finditer(window) if pattern else []:
        parts += [html.escape(window[last : match.start()]), "<mark>", html.escape(match.group()), "</mark>"]
        last = match.end()
    return "".join(parts) + html.escape(window[last:])


def freshness(published):
    """1.0 for a page from today, 0.5 after a year, 0.33 after two... and 0.5 when there is no date."""
    try:
        age_years = (date.today() - date.fromisoformat(published[:10])).days / 365
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return 0.5
    return 1 / (1 + max(age_years, 0))


class SearchEngine:
    def __init__(self, conn):
        self.conn = conn
        self.version = None
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self):
        """Reload the vectors and PageRank if `index` or `rank` changed them since the last load."""
        version = tuple(self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT (SELECT MAX(chunk_id) FROM embeddings), (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pagerank), (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sites)").fetchone())
        if version == self.version:
            return
        self.version = version
        self.vectors = embed.VectorIndex(self.conn)
        logs = {doc: math.log(score) for doc, score in self.conn.execute("SELECT doc_id, score FROM pagerank")}
        low, high = min(logs.values(), default=0.0), max(logs.values(), default=1.0)
        self.pagerank = {doc: (value - low) / ((high - low) or 1) for doc, value in logs.items()}  # 0..1
        self.sites = dict(self.conn.execute("SELECT site, score FROM sites").fetchall())

    def candidates(self, query, mode="full", site=None):
        """Best chunk per page from BM25 and/or vectors, fused with RRF. English pages only."""
        rankings = {}
        if mode in ("bm25", "hybrid", "full"):
            rankings["bm25"] = index.keyword_search(self.conn, query, config.CANDIDATES)
        if mode in ("vector", "hybrid", "full"):
            rankings["vector"] = self.vectors.search(embed.embed_query(query), config.CANDIDATES)
        fused, ranks = rrf(rankings)
        pages, seen = [], set()
        for chunk_id in sorted(fused, key=fused.get, reverse=True):
            page = dict(self.conn.execute(CHUNK_SQL, (chunk_id,)).fetchone())
            host = urlsplit(page["url"]).netloc
            if page["doc_id"] in seen or not (page["language"] or "en").startswith("en"):
                continue
            if site and host != site and not host.endswith("." + site):
                continue
            seen.add(page["doc_id"])
            pages.append({**page, "ranks": ranks[chunk_id]})
        return pages

    def rerank(self, query, pages):
        """Score each page: mostly the cross-encoder's relevance, plus authority, freshness and exact matches."""
        if not pages:
            return []
        pairs = [(query, f"{page['title']}\n{page['text']}") for page in pages]
        logits = get_reranker().predict(pairs, batch_size=16, activation_fn=torch.nn.Identity())
        words = query_words(query)
        for page, logit in zip(pages, logits):
            title_and_url = f"{page['title']} {page['url']}".lower()
            page["relevance"] = float(sigmoid(logit))
            page["pagerank"] = self.pagerank.get(page["doc_id"], 0.0)
            page["freshness"] = freshness(page["published"])
            page["exact"] = sum(word in title_and_url for word in words) / max(len(words), 1)
            page["site_authority"] = self.sites.get(discover.site_of(page["url"]), 0.0)
            page["final_score"] = (
                config.W_RELEVANCE * page["relevance"] + config.W_PAGERANK * page["pagerank"]
                + config.W_FRESHNESS * page["freshness"] + config.W_EXACT * page["exact"]
                + config.W_SITE * page["site_authority"]
            )
            if page["word_count"] < config.THIN_PAGE_WORDS:
                page["final_score"] *= config.THIN_PAGE_PENALTY
        return sorted(pages, key=lambda page: page["final_score"], reverse=True)

    def search(self, query, limit=10, offset=0, site=None, mode="full"):
        self.refresh()
        pages = self.candidates(query, mode, site)
        if mode == "full":
            pages = self.rerank(query, pages[: config.RERANK_TOP])
        return [self.to_result(page, query) for page in pages[offset : offset + limit]]

    def to_result(self, page, query):
        return {
            "url": page["url"],
            "title": page["title"] or page["url"],
            "snippet": highlight(page["text"], query),
            "relevance": page.get("relevance"),
            "final_score": page.get("final_score"),
            "signals": {
                "bm25_rank": page["ranks"].get("bm25"),
                "vector_rank": page["ranks"].get("vector"),
                "relevance": page.get("relevance"),
                "pagerank": page.get("pagerank"),
                "freshness": page.get("freshness"),
                "site_authority": page.get("site_authority"),
            },
        }
    refresh also loads the sites table, and rerank adds W_SITE × site_authority to the final score. It's a small nudge: relevance still dominates at 0.90. The new signal also appears in each result's signals.
  6. Show the new signal in the API (whole updated seekr/api.py)
    seekr/api.py
    """REST API: search Seekr from any program, with API keys and a rate limit per key."""
import hashlib
import logging
import secrets
import time
from collections import defaultdict, deque
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException, Query, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer
from pydantic import BaseModel

from seekr import config, db, embed, search
from seekr.urls import normalize_url

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
KEYS_SCHEMA = """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_keys (
    key_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, is_admin INTEGER NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL);"""


def hash_key(key):
    return hashlib.sha256(key.encode()).hexdigest()


def create_key(conn, name, admin=False):
    """Make a new random key and store only its hash. The key itself is shown once, then forgotten."""
    conn.executescript(KEYS_SCHEMA)
    key = "sk_" + secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
    created_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO api_keys VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)", (hash_key(key), name, int(admin), created_at))
    conn.commit()
    return key


class RateLimiter:
    """Allows `limit` requests per key in any 60-second window (a "sliding window")."""

    def __init__(self, limit):
        self.limit, self.hits = limit, defaultdict(deque)

    def allow(self, key, now=None):
        now = time.monotonic() if now is None else now
        hits = self.hits[key]
        while hits and hits[0] <= now - 60:
            hits.popleft()
        if len(hits) >= self.limit:
            return False
        hits.append(now)
        return True


class Signals(BaseModel):
    bm25_rank: int | None
    vector_rank: int | None
    relevance: float | None
    pagerank: float | None
    freshness: float | None
    site_authority: float | None


class Result(BaseModel):
    url: str
    title: str
    snippet: str  # HTML: page text is escaped, query words are wrapped in <mark>
    relevance: float | None
    final_score: float | None
    signals: Signals


class SearchResponse(BaseModel):
    query: str
    took_ms: int
    results: list[Result]


class Page(BaseModel):
    url: str
    title: str | None
    description: str | None
    published: str | None
    language: str | None
    word_count: int
    pagerank: float | None
    chunks: int


class Seeds(BaseModel):
    urls: list[str]


bearer = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)
router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1")


async def api_key(request: Request, credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials | None = Depends(bearer)):
    """Every endpoint depends on this: a valid key that is under its rate limit.
    All handlers are `async` so they run on one thread: a SQLite connection may only be used by the thread
    that opened it."""
    if credentials is None:
        raise HTTPException(401, "Missing API key. Send the header: Authorization: Bearer <key>")
    key_hash = hash_key(credentials.credentials)
    row = request.app.state.conn.execute("SELECT name, is_admin FROM api_keys WHERE key_hash = ?", (key_hash,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(401, "Invalid API key")
    if not request.app.state.limiter.allow(key_hash):
        raise HTTPException(429, "Rate limit exceeded, try again in a minute", headers={"Retry-After": "60"})
    return row


async def admin_key(key=Depends(api_key)):
    if not key["is_admin"]:
        raise HTTPException(403, "This endpoint needs an admin key")
    return key


@router.get("/search", response_model=SearchResponse)
async def search_pages(
    request: Request,
    q: str = Query(min_length=1, max_length=200, description="What to search for"),
    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=30),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0, le=29),
    site: str | None = Query(None, description="Only results from this host, e.g. docs.python.org"),
    key=Depends(api_key),
):
    started = time.perf_counter()
    results = request.app.state.engine.search(q, limit=limit, offset=offset, site=site)
    took_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000)
    log.info("search %r offset=%d site=%s -> %d results in %d ms", q, offset, site, len(results), took_ms)
    return {"query": q, "took_ms": took_ms, "results": results}


@router.get("/page", response_model=Page)
async def page_info(request: Request, url: str, key=Depends(api_key)):
    row = request.app.state.conn.execute(
        """SELECT d.url, d.title, d.description, d.published, d.language, d.word_count, r.score AS pagerank,
                  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks c WHERE c.doc_id = d.id) AS chunks
           FROM documents d LEFT JOIN pagerank r ON r.doc_id = d.id WHERE d.url = ? AND d.duplicate_of IS NULL""",
        (url,),
    ).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "That URL is not in the index")
    return dict(row)


@router.get("/stats")
async def stats(request: Request, key=Depends(api_key)):
    count = lambda sql: request.app.state.conn.execute(sql).fetchone()[0]  # noqa: E731
    return {
        "pages": count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pages"),
        "documents": count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM documents WHERE duplicate_of IS NULL AND word_count > 0"),
        "chunks": count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks"),
        "embeddings": count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM embeddings"),
        "device": embed.best_device(),
    }


@router.post("/crawl/seeds")
async def add_seeds(body: Seeds, key=Depends(admin_key)):
    """Add start URLs for the next crawl. Crawling itself runs from the command line."""
    urls = [normalize_url(url) for url in body.urls]
    if None in urls:
        raise HTTPException(422, "Every seed must be an http(s) URL")
    known = set(config.read_seeds())
    added = [url for url in dict.fromkeys(urls) if url not in known]
    with open(config.SEEDS_FILE, "a") as seeds_file:
        seeds_file.writelines(f"{url}\n" for url in added)
    return {"added": added, "next": "python -m seekr crawl && python -m seekr index && python -m seekr rank"}


def create_app(db_path=None):
    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app):
        app.state.conn = db.connect(db_path)
        app.state.conn.executescript(KEYS_SCHEMA)
        app.state.engine = search.SearchEngine(app.state.conn)  # loads vectors + models once, at startup
        app.state.engine.search("warm up")  # the GPU compiles its kernels on first use; do it before users arrive
        app.state.limiter = RateLimiter(config.RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE)
        yield
        app.state.conn.close()

    app = FastAPI(title="Seekr API", version="0.1", lifespan=lifespan)
    app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=config.CORS_ORIGINS, allow_methods=["GET", "POST"],
                       allow_headers=["Authorization", "Content-Type"])
    app.include_router(router)
    return app
    One new field in the Signals model: site_authority.
  7. Add discover and make live run forever (whole updated seekr/__main__.py)
    seekr/__main__.py
    """Command-line entry point: python -m seekr <command>."""
import argparse
import asyncio
import logging
import sys
import threading
import time

from seekr import api, config, crawler, db, discover, doctor, embed, evaluate, extract, index, rank, search, web


def print_host_stats(conn):
    print(f"{'host':26} {'done':>5} {'queued':>7} {'failed':>7} {'error':>6} {'blocked':>8}")
    for r in db.host_stats(conn):
        print(f"{r['host']:26} {r['done']:5} {r['queued']:7} {r['failed']:7} {r['error']:6} {r['blocked']:8}")


def cmd_crawl(args):
    conn = db.connect()
    asyncio.run(crawler.crawl(config.read_seeds() + discover.found_seeds(conn), conn))
    print_host_stats(conn)


def cmd_index(args):
    conn = db.connect()
    print("Extracting text from crawled pages:")
    counts = extract.index_pages(conn)
    print(f"new: {counts['new']}  duplicates: {counts['duplicate']}  no main text: {counts['empty']}")
    print(f"Keyword index: added {index.build_index(conn)} chunks")
    print(f"Embedding chunks on {embed.best_device()}:")
    print(f"embedded: {embed.embed_chunks(conn)}")


def cmd_rank(args):
    conn = db.connect()
    pages, links = rank.compute_pagerank(conn)
    print(f"PageRank over {pages} pages and {links} links. Top 10:")
    top = conn.execute(
        "SELECT r.score, d.url FROM pagerank r JOIN documents d ON d.id = r.doc_id ORDER BY r.score DESC LIMIT 10"
    )
    for row in top:
        print(f"  {row['score']:.5f}  {row['url']}")


def cmd_discover(args):
    conn = db.connect()
    print(f"Site importance: {discover.compute_sites(conn)} sites are linked from our pages")
    for seed in discover.discover(conn, config.read_seeds()):
        print(f"  new site to crawl: {seed}")


def crawl_forever():
    """Crawl in rounds of CRAWL_ROUND seconds, so each round also crawls the sites discovered meanwhile."""
    while True:
        conn = db.connect()
        before = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pages").fetchone()[0]
        seeds = config.read_seeds() + discover.found_seeds(conn)
        asyncio.run(crawler.crawl(seeds, conn, until=time.monotonic() + config.CRAWL_ROUND))
        if conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pages").fetchone()[0] == before:
            time.sleep(config.LIVE_INTERVAL)  # nothing new to fetch: wait for discover to find more sites


def cmd_live(args):
    """Crawl forever in a background thread. Every LIVE_INTERVAL: index, rank and discover new sites."""
    crawling = threading.Thread(target=crawl_forever, daemon=True)
    crawling.start()
    while True:
        cmd_index(args)
        cmd_rank(args)
        cmd_discover(args)
        if not crawling.is_alive():
            break
        crawling.join(timeout=config.LIVE_INTERVAL)  # wait, but wake up early if the crawl thread stops


def cmd_search(args):
    engine = search.SearchEngine(db.connect())
    for i, result in enumerate(engine.search(" ".join(args.query)), start=1):
        print(f"{i:2}. [{result['relevance']:.2f}] {result['title']}\n    {result['url']}")


def cmd_eval(args):
    engine = search.SearchEngine(db.connect())
    queries = evaluate.load_queries()
    print(f"{len(queries)} queries   {'MRR@10':>7} {'nDCG@10':>8}")
    for mode, (mrr, ndcg) in evaluate.evaluate(engine, queries).items():
        print(f"{mode:18} {mrr:7.3f} {ndcg:8.3f}")


def cmd_serve(args):
    import uvicorn

    print(f"Seekr on http://{config.HOST}:{config.PORT}  (search page: /, API docs: /docs)")
    uvicorn.run(web.create_site(), host=config.HOST, port=config.PORT)


def cmd_keys(args):
    key = api.create_key(db.connect(), args.name, admin=args.admin)
    print(f"New {'admin ' if args.admin else ''}API key for {args.name!r} (shown once, store it safely):\n{key}")


def cmd_doctor(args):
    sys.exit(0 if doctor.run_checks() else 1)


def cmd_stats(args):
    seeds = config.read_seeds()
    db_state = "exists" if config.DB_PATH.exists() else "not created yet"
    print(f"seeds:    {len(seeds)}")
    print(f"database: {config.DB_PATH} ({db_state})")
    if config.DB_PATH.exists():
        conn = db.connect()
        print_host_stats(conn)
        for table in ("pages", "documents", "chunks", "embeddings"):
            print(f"{table + ':':10} {conn.execute(f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table}').fetchone()[0]}")


COMMANDS = {
    "crawl": cmd_crawl,
    "index": cmd_index,
    "rank": cmd_rank,
    "discover": cmd_discover,
    "live": cmd_live,
    "serve": cmd_serve,
    "stats": cmd_stats,
    "search": cmd_search,
    "eval": cmd_eval,
    "keys": cmd_keys,
    "doctor": cmd_doctor,
}


def main(argv=None):
    logging.basicConfig(level=config.LOG_LEVEL, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)-7s %(name)s: %(message)s",
                        datefmt="%H:%M:%S")
    for noisy in ("sentence_transformers", "httpx", "httpx2"):  # progress bars and one line per HTTP request
        logging.getLogger(noisy).setLevel(logging.WARNING)
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="seekr", description="Seekr search engine")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for name in COMMANDS:
        command = sub.add_parser(name)
        if name == "search":
            command.add_argument("query", nargs="+")
        if name == "keys":
            command.add_argument("action", choices=["create"])
            command.add_argument("name", help="who or what the key is for")
            command.add_argument("--admin", action="store_true", help="may add seeds")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    COMMANDS[args.command](args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
    • cmd_crawl crawls the discovered seeds too.
    • cmd_discover rebuilds site importance and adds new sites.
    • crawl_forever crawls in rounds, reading the seed list again each round. If a round fetches nothing, because every site is finished, it waits LIVE_INTERVAL instead of spinning in a tight loop.
    • cmd_live now runs index → rank → discover, then waits. So Seekr keeps growing on its own: crawled pages lead to new sites, which lead to more pages.
  8. Write the discovery tests
    tests/test_discover.py
    import asyncio
import time

import httpx

from seekr import crawler, db, discover

# Fake sites for the discovery checks: host -> {path: (status, body)}
SITES = {
    "good.org": {"/robots.txt": (404, ""), "/": (200, '<html lang="en"><title>Good</title>')},
    "closed.com": {"/robots.txt": (200, "User-agent: *\nDisallow: /"), "/": (200, "<html>")},
    "french.fr": {"/robots.txt": (404, ""), "/": (200, '<html lang="fr"><title>Bonjour</title>')},
}


def fake_web(request):
    status, body = SITES.get(request.url.host, {}).get(request.url.path, (404, ""))
    return httpx.Response(status, headers={"content-type": "text/html"}, text=body)


def add_page(conn, url, links):
    db.save_page(conn, url, url, 200, "", "", links)


def test_sites_linked_from_many_other_sites_score_higher(tmp_path):
    conn = db.connect(tmp_path / "t.db")
    add_page(conn, "https://www.a.com/1", ["https://popular.org/", "https://a.com/2"])  # a.com -> a.com doesn't count
    add_page(conn, "https://a.com/3", ["https://popular.org/x"])  # the same site twice still counts once
    add_page(conn, "https://b.com/", ["https://popular.org/", "https://rare.org/"])
    assert discover.compute_sites(conn) == 2  # popular.org and rare.org
    sites = {row[0]: (row[1], row[2]) for row in conn.execute("SELECT site, linked_from, score FROM sites")}
    assert sites["popular.org"] == (2, 1.0)
    assert sites["rare.org"][0] == 1 and 0 < sites["rare.org"][1] < 1
    assert "a.com" not in sites


def test_discover_checks_robots_and_language_and_remembers(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(discover.config, "DISCOVER_MIN_LINKS", 2)
    conn = db.connect(tmp_path / "t.db")
    for site in ("good.org", "closed.com", "french.fr", "facebook.com", "seed.com", "lonely.net"):
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sites VALUES (?, ?, 0.5)", (site, 1 if site == "lonely.net" else 5))
    transport = httpx.MockTransport(fake_web)
    added = discover.discover(conn, ["https://www.seed.com/"], transport=transport)
    assert added == ["https://good.org/"]  # closed.com: robots; french.fr: language; facebook: skip list
    assert discover.found_seeds(conn) == ["https://good.org/"]
    assert discover.discover(conn, ["https://www.seed.com/"], transport=transport) == []  # nothing is checked twice


def test_crawl_stops_at_its_deadline(tmp_path):
    conn = db.connect(tmp_path / "t.db")
    transport = httpx.MockTransport(fake_web)
    asyncio.run(crawler.crawl(["https://good.org/"], conn, transport=transport, until=time.monotonic()))
    assert conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pages").fetchone()[0] == 0
    A fake mini-web (httpx.MockTransport, as in Chapter 2): one good English site, one that disallows bots, one in French, and Facebook from the skip list. Only the good site is added, and a second run checks nothing, because every answer was remembered.
  9. Update the live test (whole updated tests/test_live.py)
    tests/test_live.py
    import time

from seekr import __main__ as cli
from seekr import config, db, embed, extract, rank, search


def add_page(conn, doc_id, text):
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO documents (id, url, title, language, word_count, published) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'en', ?, '')",
                 (doc_id, f"https://x.com/{doc_id}", text, len(text.split())))
    conn.execute("INSERT INTO chunks (id, doc_id, position, text) VALUES (?, ?, 0, ?)", (doc_id, doc_id, text))


def test_database_can_be_read_while_another_program_writes(tmp_path):
    writer, reader = db.connect(tmp_path / "t.db"), db.connect(tmp_path / "t.db")
    assert writer.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode").fetchone()[0] == "wal"
    db.enqueue(writer, ["https://a.com/"], depth=0)  # an open, uncommitted write
    assert reader.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM frontier").fetchone()[0] == 0  # readers aren't blocked


def test_search_engine_picks_up_new_embeddings(tmp_path):
    conn = db.connect(tmp_path / "t.db")
    conn.execute(rank.SCHEMA)
    add_page(conn, 1, "Rust ownership rules")
    embed.embed_chunks(conn)
    engine = search.SearchEngine(conn)
    assert engine.vectors.chunk_ids == [1]
    add_page(conn, 2, "Python asyncio event loop")
    embed.embed_chunks(conn)
    engine.refresh()
    assert engine.vectors.chunk_ids == [1, 2]


def test_live_indexes_while_the_crawl_runs(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "crawl_forever", lambda: time.sleep(0.5))  # a fake crawl that ends quickly
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "cmd_index", lambda args: calls.append("index"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "cmd_rank", lambda args: calls.append("rank"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "cmd_discover", lambda args: calls.append("discover"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "LIVE_INTERVAL", 0.1)
    cli.main(["live"])
    assert calls[:3] == ["index", "rank", "discover"]  # right away, then again every LIVE_INTERVAL
    assert len(calls) >= 9


def test_one_broken_page_does_not_stop_indexing(monkeypatch):
    def broken(html, url, page_title=""):
        raise ValueError("Invalid \\escape")

    monkeypatch.setattr(extract, "extract", broken)
    assert extract.extract_row(("https://x.com/", "<html>", "https://x.com/", "")) is None
    Important: the test replaces crawl_forever with a fake. When we first forgot this, the test started a real crawl that never ended. A test must never touch the network or the real database.

    import numpy as np
import pytest

from seekr import db, embed, index, rank, search


def test_rrf_rewards_agreement():
    fused, ranks = search.rrf({"bm25": [("a", 9), ("b", 5)], "vector": [("b", 0.9), ("c", 0.8)]})
    assert max(fused, key=fused.get) == "b"  # 2nd + 1st beats a single 1st place
    assert ranks["b"] == {"bm25": 2, "vector": 1}
    assert np.isclose(fused["a"], 1 / 61)

    assert "<mark>Tasks</mark>" in snippet


def test_freshness_fades_with_age():

    assert search.freshness("2000-01-01") < search.freshness("2020-01-01") < 1

    assert search.sigmoid(-10) < 0.001 < 0.999 < search.sigmoid(10)


PAGES = {

                                     "goes out of scope, so it needs no garbage collector. " * 3),
    "https://x.com/cooking": ("Pasta recipes", "Boil water, add salt and cook the pasta for nine minutes. " * 3),

                              "verlässt. " * 3),
}


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def engine(tmp_path_factory):
    conn = db.connect(tmp_path_factory.mktemp("s") / "t.db")
    for doc_id, (url, (title, text)) in enumerate(PAGES.items(), start=1):
        language = "de" if "/de/" in url else "en"
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO documents (id, url, title, language, word_count, published) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, '')",
                     (doc_id, url, title, language, len(text.split())))
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO chunks (id, doc_id, position, text) VALUES (?, ?, 0, ?)", (doc_id, doc_id, text))
        db.save_page(conn, url, url, 200, title, "", list(PAGES))
    index.build_index(conn)
    embed.embed_chunks(conn)
    rank.compute_pagerank(conn)
    return search.SearchEngine(conn)

def test_contact_goes_into_the_user_agent(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("SEEKR_CONTACT", "Ada Lovelace; ada@example.com")
    try:
        importlib.reload(config)
        assert config.USER_AGENT == "SeekrBot/0.1 (Ada Lovelace; ada@example.com)"
    finally:
        monkeypatch.delenv("SEEKR_CONTACT")
        importlib.reload(config)
    assert config.USER_AGENT == "SeekrBot/0.1"
    importlib.reload(config) runs config.py again, so it reads the new environment variable. The finally block reloads it once more without the variable, so the other tests see normal settings.
